Shares the facet contract with UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1242; parallels UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#63/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#64 (afk).

## 2026-07-11 — Bio set ↔ `people` connection is single-source-of-truth (hide manual fields on connect) (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1418)

**Decision.** The optional `people`-entry connection on the Bio set is single-source-of-truth: when a person is connected, conditional-hide the manual image/text fields (the person entry wins). Use `portrait` with `portrait_wide` fallback, a **hardcoded** `select` of the 12 known `bio*` handles (default `bio`), pull bio + portrait only, and render nothing for an empty selected bio. Additive/optional — an unconnected block renders exactly as UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1169 ships. No extended contract (name/credentials/role) for now.

**Why.** The ticket exists because copy-pasted bio text drifts from the person's canonical bio; hiding the manual fields on connect is the only option that actually eliminates that drift. A hardcoded 12-item selector avoids coupling set config to the blueprint shape for a rarely-changing list.

**Rejected.** *Override model* (manual fields as per-block overrides falling back to person data) — reintroduces the duplicate-then-drift problem the ticket is trying to eliminate. *Blueprint-derived selector* — DRY but couples set config to blueprint shape (cheap follow-up if `bio*` variants churn). *Extended contract* (name/credentials/role heading) — widens the block's surface; defer.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1418 issue `## Decision`; `resources/fieldsets/uamsweb_block_bio.yaml`, `resources/views/page_builder/_uamsweb_block_bio.antlers.html`, `resources/blueprints/collections/people/person.yaml`. Base Bio set UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1169 (shipped) untouched.

## 2026-07-11 — Custom page-builder-set anchor: slugify + reject-on-collision + replace the auto id (#1214)

**Decision.** Add an optional custom anchor field on page-builder sets: `Str::slug` the input, a save-time uniqueness rule rejects duplicate anchors on the same page, and a custom id fully replaces the auto `module-<setId>`. A set with no custom id still emits the auto id unchanged. Plumbs through `block_id` in the one shared wrapper (`_uamsweb_block.antlers.html`, which emits `{{ block_id | attribute:id }}` from the `page_builder_block_auto_id` tag).

**Why.** The feature exists to give editors a durable, typeable anchor, so the emitted id must equal what they typed (rules out silent `-2`/`-3` suffixing) and be valid/unique HTML (rules out duplicate ids). A save-time uniqueness rule surfaces collisions to the editor instead of silently mangling or breaking in-page jumps.

**Rejected.** *Slugify + auto-suffix duplicates at render* — never errors but the emitted anchor can differ from what was typed, weakening the durable/typeable promise. *Slugify + last-wins + keep the auto id as fallback* — best back-compat but duplicate custom ids are invalid HTML and only the first is honored.

**Companion.** #1214 issue `## Decision`; `resources/views/page_builder/_uamsweb_block.antlers.html`, `app/Tags/PageBuilderBlockAutoId.php`, `resources/fieldsets/uamsweb_common_block.yaml`. Mirrors the existing Bard heading `anchor`. Residual: cross-set uniqueness needs a custom rule/save listener (not native to blueprint validation).

## 2026-07-11 — Location alert windows: two optional `date` fields evaluated by a PHP computed property (#1212)

**Decision.** Model the optional alert scheduling window as two optional `date` fields (time enabled) — start and end — on both the per-location alert and the system-wide alert, evaluated by a PHP computed-property provider: fixed `America/Chicago`, inclusive bounds, single bound = open-ended. Outside the window the alert renders "No modification"; no window set = always active (today's behavior).

**Why.** Only this shape satisfies both pinned constraints at once — open-ended before/after windows *and* the mandated inside/outside/no-window test coverage — while centralizing DST/timezone in one unit-tested place. The existing alert model (`alert_modification` + heading/body/color and the global equivalents) is untouched.

**Rejected.** *Inline Antlers evaluation with date modifiers* — fewer PHP files but timezone/DST comparison in templates is error-prone, duplicated per render site, and hard to test to the AC. *Single `date` field in `mode: range`* — compact CP UI but Statamic range mode requires BOTH bounds, so it cannot express the required open-ended case.

**Companion.** #1212 issue `## Decision`; `resources/blueprints/collections/clinical_locations/location.yaml` (alert tab), `resources/blueprints/globals/uams_fad_settings.yaml`. The actual alert merge/evaluation site lives in the `vendor/uams-web` theme — the evaluator wires in there.

## 2026-07-11 — Merge `excerpt`/`meta_description` into one required `excerpt` on every routable blueprint (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1378)

**Decision.** Retire `meta_description`; make `excerpt` the single required page summary on the routable set (`pages`, `posts`, `people`, `knowledge_bases`, `clinical_expertise`). Rewrite `_seo_meta.antlers.html` from `$seo_description = meta_description ? meta_description : excerpt` to `= excerpt`, drop `meta_description` from the 6 blueprints, and fold any distinct `meta_description` values into `excerpt` now (pre-final-migration #696). For `people/person` (which has `bio_short` but no `excerpt`), reuse `bio_short` as the resolved summary — wire the person head so `$seo_description` falls to it — rather than adding a required `excerpt` field to every person.

**Why.** The ticket's purpose is to guarantee every routable page has a non-empty summary; merging makes that structurally true and removes the two-near-identical-fields confusion. Now — pre-final-migration — is the cheapest moment to fold values. `excerpt` is the pinned canonical handle (~10 templates consume it).

**Rejected.** *Keep both fields with only `excerpt` required* — preserves a SERP-tuned description distinct from the card blurb but keeps two fields editors must distinguish and asymmetric coverage; the distinct-description need is rare here and can return as an optional field later. *For people: a dedicated required `excerpt` field* — cleaner separation and uniform, but burdens every person entry and may need a content-owner call (spin-off if desired).

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1378 issue `## Decision`; `resources/views/layout/header/_seo_meta.antlers.html`, the 6 routable blueprints, `resources/fieldsets/uamsweb_common.yaml`. `post_excerpt→excerpt` (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#800, closed) adopted, not re-performed; planned-public collections inherit the rule in their own front-end tickets.

## 2026-07-11 — Site search: one standalone `/search/results` page over the existing local index (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#506, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#629)

**Superseded in part (2026-07-13, above).** The endpoint is now **`/search`** (not `/search/results`) and the page carries a relevance-ranked list + a type-filter dropdown with per-type cards. The **`local`-driver + unified-`default`-index core** and the **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#506 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#629** decisions in this entry stand.

**Decision.** Build a single standalone `/search/results` route/controller/page powered by the in-repo `local` search driver over the existing unified `default` content index (`config/statamic/search.php`), serving both site-wide and knowledge-base search, scoped by query param. Per-ticket: **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815** — `local` + unified index, engine-agnostic results layer. **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56** — the standalone page (not a header-tray form); it is the endpoint the KB search bar already posts to. **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890** — remove the now-redundant empty header search tray (`#toggle-search` / `#header-search`) as the a11y fix. **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#506** — KB search is a `knowledge_bases` local index with the topic scope applied as a bounded subtree id-set intersection (reusing `app/Scopes/KnowledgeBase*`). **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#629** — the 404 page gets an inline `/search/results` box + a short main-nav-sourced popular-links list.

**Why.** The `local` `default` index and the `/search/results` target (already hard-coded in the KB `_search_bar.antlers.html` `action`) both exist — one standalone page ships the entire cluster with zero infra/cost, unifies site + KB search on one endpoint, gives real results-UX room (pagination, zero-state, focus), and makes the empty header tray redundant. Basic relevance is an accepted MVP gap; the engine-agnostic UI means a later hosted-engine upgrade is a backend swap, not a rewrite.

**Rejected.** *Hosted engine* (Algolia/Meilisearch) — best relevance/scale but provisioning + cost + env wiring, over-provisioned for an MVP corpus (revisitable post-launch behind the same UI). *local + per-collection merge* — app-side merge/ranking complexity for no launch benefit. *Inline header-tray search form* (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56) — cramped overlay, focus-trap burden, diverges from the KB bar's `/search/results` target. *Neutralize-but-keep the empty tray* (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890) — leaves dead, forgettable code. *KB scoped-tree-query only* (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#506) — no relevance ranking. *404 curated-links-only / overlay-reuse* (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#629) — weaker wayfinding.

**Companion.** Issues UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#506/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#629 `## Decision`; `config/statamic/search.php`, `resources/views/knowledge_bases/_search_bar.antlers.html`, `resources/views/layout/search/_search-tray.antlers.html`, `resources/views/errors/404.antlers.html`. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56 retains its build-order `blocked_by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815` (needs the endpoint). Suppressed/empty-topic exclusion per UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#215/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#216.

## 2026-07-11 — Clinical-subsite content pages emit generic `WebPage` + `isPartOf` the subsite hub (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1414)

**Decision.** `basic_page`/`landing_page` entries inside the `clinical_expertise` and `clinical_locations` subsites emit one shared shape: `@type: WebPage` + a per-entry `isPartOf` resolved to the nearest subsite hub (`MedicalSpecialty` / `MedicalClinic`) via the existing ancestor walk, pushed through the collections' `inject.schema_templates.blueprint_defaults`.

**Why.** The load-bearing SEO signal is the `isPartOf` hub linkage, not the page's own `@type`. Most of these pages are marketing/program/contact pages, so `WebPage` is the *honest* type — typing them `MedicalWebPage` would over-claim and dilute the medical signal. The ancestor walk (`ClinicalExpertiseComputedFieldsService`) and `blueprint_defaults` inheritance already exist, so this is zero editor burden on production infrastructure.

**Rejected.** *`MedicalWebPage` for expertise / `WebPage` for locations* — best hub fit but over-claims on non-clinical pages and adds a second default to keep drift-free. *Editor-selectable subtype discriminator* (the `schema_*_subtype` pattern) — most accurate per page but adds fields + per-entry authoring tax for near-uniform pages (runner-up if genuinely medical body pages appear). *Computed-from-parent as the primary mechanism* — same shape as chosen but couples page schema to structure-tree position with no honesty gain over `blueprint_defaults`.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1414 issue `## Decision`; `resources/blueprints/collections/clinical_{expertise,locations}/{basic_page,landing_page}.yaml` `blueprint_defaults`; ancestor walk in `ClinicalExpertiseComputedFieldsService`. Coordinate with UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1065 so page-level `about`/`mainEntity` isn't double-emitted; blueprint pairs kept drift-free per UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1068.

## 2026-07-11 — Unify the schema-type query scopes on `Str::snake` handles under one prefix (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1408)

**Decision.** Collapse `EntriesBySchemaType` + `EntriesByResolvedSchemaType` into one dual-mode scope whose aliases are `Str::snake($case->value)` under a single `entries_by_schema_type` prefix, then delete the redundant class. This fixes the `administrative_area` silent no-op.

**Why.** The two siblings encoded type aliases *differently* — slug (`administrativearea`, from `SchemaTypeInfo::slug`) vs snake (`administrative_area`, from `Str::snake`) — so a snake handle referenced against the slug-keyed scope matched nothing (silent no-op). Standardizing on `Str::snake` fixes the bug touching only alias generation + `ScopeAliasCache` + the `GenerateSchemaTypeEnum` hook, leaves the live `schema_objects` picker blueprints untouched, and gives the most readable handles; only the clinical `resolved_schema_type_*` handles re-point.

**Rejected.** *Slug format under one prefix* — fixes the bug but edits both `schema_objects` and clinical blueprints for a less-readable convention. *Keep both prefixes, dispatch by prefix* — zero churn but leaves two conventions coexisting and does **not** fix the no-op. *Reopening the two-sibling design from UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1051/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1052* — settled; this only merges them.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1408 issue `## Decision`; `app/Scopes/EntriesBySchemaType.php`, `app/Scopes/EntriesByResolvedSchemaType.php`, `app/Enums/SchemaType.php`, `app/Cache/ScopeAliasCache.php`, `app/Console/Commands/GenerateSchemaTypeEnum.php`.

## 2026-07-11 — Remove all six dead/unwired query scopes surfaced by the scope audit (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1368)

**Decision.** Delete all six audit-flagged scopes — `ExcludeSiblings`, `ExcludeAncestors`, `KnowledgeBaseTopicChildren`, `ValidTopicParent`, `ClinicalMembershipOrganization`, `ClinicalCertification` — with their tests/registrations/Rules. `App\Support\ContentAncestorIds` is retained (live callers: `SubsectionResolver`, `FacilityInheritedAddressService`).

**Why.** None are referenced by any blueprint/config `query_scopes` today; deleting shrinks the CP `query_scopes` option list and the maintenance surface. The two that encode real latent features — a KB **subtopic** listing (mirror of the live `KnowledgeBaseArticleChildren`) and a clinical/non-clinical **split** on the membership-org picker (`clinical_query` field exists) — are cheap, precedented re-adds if product later asks. `ValidTopicParent` and `ClinicalCertification` are broken/false-docblock, so wiring them would need a fix first regardless.

**Rejected.** *Wire `KnowledgeBaseTopicChildren` and/or `ClinicalMembershipOrganization` now* — builds a feature nobody has requested; deferred to a product ask. *Selective per-scope mix* — same, plus the broken-docblock caveat. The `UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1051` flat `SchemaType*` family stays out of scope (owned there; see UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1408).

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1368 issue `## Decision`; `app/Scopes/` (six deletions), `app/Providers/ValidationServiceProvider.php` registrations.

## 2026-07-11 — Region-overlap provider scope filters via resolved location IDs, not a denormalized field (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1366)

**Decision.** Rewrite `OntologyOverlapRegions` to resolve the block's region term slugs → `clinical_locations` entry IDs (querying locations whose authoritative `regions` overlap the slugs), then mutate the builder in place with `whereJsonOverlaps('clinical_locations', $ids)` — never `$query->get()`-then-filter-and-return. The `locations` → `clinical_locations` handle correction in `OntologyOverlapLocations` ships with it.

**Why.** Statamic's `Scope::apply()` is `@return void`; the collection-tag ([`QueriesScopes.php:18`](../vendor/statamic/cms/src/Tags/Concerns/QueriesScopes.php)) and relationship call sites **discard** the return, so the old filter-in-PHP-and-return-a-new-`EntryCollection` was a silent no-op (returned every provider). `regions` is a computed-only aggregate on providers/people, so it can't be a `where` target directly. Resolving through `clinical_locations` keeps `regions` authoritative on locations (single source of truth, no drift) at the cost of one bounded lookup query.

**Rejected.** *Denormalize a stored `regions` field onto providers/people* (compute-on-save + backfill) — symmetric with the sibling `whereJsonOverlaps` scopes and one fast filter, but adds a stored field plus sync machinery and a standing drift bug when a location's region changes without re-saving every linked provider. Not worth it for a tiny region cardinality.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1366 issue `## Decision`; `app/Scopes/OntologyOverlapRegions.php`, `app/Scopes/OntologyOverlapLocations.php` (reference pattern: `OntologyOverlapExpertise`). Fixes a latent defect ahead of its first live render consumer (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#64/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919).

## 2026-07-12 — Clinical-provider directory: mount + eligible-listing core now, routed sections split to #1475 (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070)

**Decision.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070 ships the `/providers` mount + eligible-provider directory core: `people` mounts to a dedicated `/providers` `pages` landing (the existing `people/{slug}` route and non-clinical multi-site `people` are unchanged), which renders `people/index` → `people/_listing` — the clinical-provider-profile-eligible, searchable subset as a card grid, reusing UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919's `clinical_provider_profile_eligible_query:is="true" searchable:is="true"` conditions. Flat (D8): no descendant blueprints, no tree validation, no depth-gate. The per-provider routed `/{section}` subpages + subbrand/breadcrumb chrome (AC4 / AC5) split to #1475 (blocked by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070).

**Why.** The AoE section-routing subsystem the routed-`/{section}` treatment reuses is not collection-agnostic (hardcoded to `clinical_expertise`), so AC4 / AC5 mean generalizing a shared, recently-shipped subsystem to `people` — a materially larger, AoE-regression-sensitive change than the mount + listing. Splitting delivers the directory core (the product win) cleanly and isolates the risky generalization into #1475 for its own review. The public listing filters eligible **and** searchable (not the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405 picker's eligibility-only) to match UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919's directory pattern.

**Rendering.** The mount does not auto-serve a `{collection}/index` view — `DataResponse` renders the landing entry's own `template` — so the landing names `people/index` explicitly. (The sibling `clinical_resources` / `clinical_expertise` / `clinical_locations` landings use `template: pages/page_landing` and so do not render their collection `index` at the mount URL — a separate potential gap, noted in #1475's out-of-scope.)

**Rejected.** *Full UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070 in one PR* (generalize the AoE subsystem now) — larger diff + real AoE-regression surface in a mixed PR, harder to review; deferred to #1475. *Descope UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070 entirely* — forgoes the buildable directory core the group waited through the U→T chain for. *Filter the listing eligibility-only (reuse UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405's picker scope)* — a public directory should hide separated / hidden providers, matching UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919; use the eligible + searchable tag conditions. *Rely on mount auto-index* — Statamic renders the landing entry's own template, not a collection index.

**Companion.** `resources/views/people/{index,_listing}.antlers.html`, `content/collections/people.yaml` (mount), `content/collections/pages/health/providers.md`, `content/trees/collections/health/pages.yaml`, `tests/Feature/Views/ProvidersListingTest.php`, `tests/Feature/PeopleProvidersMountTest.php`; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070 issue `## Decision`; follow-up #1475.

## 2026-07-12 — CP-picker provider eligibility: in-memory `query_scopes`, eligibility-only, no stored index (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405)

**Decision.** Constrain the `uamsweb_common_fad.providers` ("Related Clinical Providers") picker to clinical-provider-profile-eligible `people` via a new `App\Scopes\ClinicalProviderEligiblePeople` query scope listed as a `query_scopes` handle on that field. The scope resolves the eligible id set in memory — reusing UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919's `ProviderPeopleListingComputedValues::computeClinicalProviderProfileEligibleQuery()` predicate — and constrains the picker with `whereIn('id', …)`. It filters on eligibility **only**, not `searchable`.

**Why.** Resolves the CP-picker fork UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 left to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405 ("direct in-memory `where` vs. a persisted/indexed mirror"). Eligibility is a computed flag (not stored/indexed) and the eligible set is a small subset of `people`, so an in-memory id resolve on the file/Stache driver is correct and cheap — no persisted mirror to build or keep in sync. Reusing the exact UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 predicate satisfies AC #2 (no divergent re-evaluation): the picker and the live directories agree on who is a provider. Eligibility-only (not `searchable`) so an editor can still manage associations for a temporarily-separated / hidden provider; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919's output filter — not the picker — keeps them out of the public render.

**Rejected.** *Persisted / indexed eligibility mirror* — unnecessary complexity plus a sync surface on the file/Stache driver for a small set; revisit only on a SQL/eloquent driver migration (the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 driver caveat). *`where('clinical_provider_profile_eligible_query', true)` on the Stache* — computed values aren't indexed, so a column `where` cannot express it. *Fold in `searchable`* — would block editors from associating temporarily-separated providers, conflating the editorial pool with the public-render filter.

**Companion.** `app/Scopes/ClinicalProviderEligiblePeople.php`, `resources/fieldsets/uamsweb_common_fad.yaml` (providers field), `tests/Feature/Scopes/ClinicalProviderEligiblePeopleTest.php`; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405 issue `## Decision`. Resolves the CP-picker leg of the 2026-07-12 UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 decision below.

## 2026-07-12 — Bound the stillat RelationshipProcessor with an in-repo subclass, not a vendor patch (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1406)

**Decision.** Bound the shared `stillat/relationships` processor with an in-repo `App\Support\Relationships\BoundedRelationshipProcessor` (extends the vendor `RelationshipProcessor`) that resets the four per-operation working sets (`effectedEntries` / `effectedUsers` / `effectedTerms` / `dependencies`) at the start of every `processRelationship()`, registered as a container singleton over `RelationshipProcessor::class` in `StillatRelationshipsServiceProvider::register()`.

**Why.** The vendor processor is a singleton whose working sets are appended via array `+=` and never reset, so the two bulk-save hot paths — the WordPress importer and `php please relate:fill` — grow it unbounded (memory) and, because `+=` keeps the value already stored under an id, serve a *stale* snapshot when an entity cached early is re-touched later in the same process (a snapshot clobber if it was edited in between). All four processing traits (`processManyToMany/ManyToOne/OneToOne/OneToMany`) read only the ids of the relationship currently being processed — the ones `processRelationship()` just appended — and the reciprocal-save cascade is blocked from re-entering by the addon's `isProcessingRelationships()` guard, so resetting per `processRelationship` is safe and fixes both paths at once, including `relate:fill` (which calls `processRelationship()` directly and bypasses `process()`). The vendor manager resolves its processor via `$app->make(RelationshipProcessor::class)`, so the subclass binds with no vendor code change; an `instanceof` test guards the wiring against a future vendor change.

**Rejected.** *Suppress sync during import* (`withoutEvents` / a flag around importer saves) — the importer is a separate package (cross-repo), and it leaves `relate:fill` unbounded. *Chunk + reset `relate:fill`* — needs a reset primitive anyway and never touches the importer save path. *`cweagans/composer-patches` vendor patch* — same effect but a patch file to rebaseline on every `stillat/relationships` bump; the subclass is in-repo, unit-testable, and survives upgrades.

**Companion.** `app/Support/Relationships/BoundedRelationshipProcessor.php`, `app/Providers/StillatRelationshipsServiceProvider.php`, `tests/Feature/Relationships/BoundedRelationshipProcessorTest.php`; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1406 issue `## Decision`. Bounds the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1360 stillat backfill surface (2026-07-07 entry).

## 2026-07-12 — Provider-relationship eligibility filter: one shared modifier at the live directories; schema `about` + CP-picker deferred (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919)

**Decision.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 filters provider relationships to clinical-provider-eligible, searchable `people` at every **live provider directory**: the FaD Providers block (`{{ collection:people … searchable:is="true" clinical_provider_profile_eligible_query:is="true" }}`, mirroring the existing `searchable:is` pattern — the block stays gated for UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1242), and the three authored `uamsweb_common_fad.providers` relationship renders — location "Providers at This Location" (`physician_locations`), AoE overview + routed section "Related Clinical Providers" (`clinical_providers`), and clinical-resource "Related Providers" (`clinical_providers`) — via **one shared `clinical_provider_eligible` Antlers modifier** (`app/Modifiers/`) reusing `ProviderPeopleListingComputedValues::{computeClinicalProviderProfileEligibleQuery,computeSearchable}`, the exact predicate the block's two tag conditions express. No hard, save-blocking validation is added (AC #5).

**Why.** The predicate lives once (`ClinicalProviderProfileEligibility` + `config/clinical_provider_profile.php`) and is applied through the same two computeds everywhere, so a person listed as a provider anywhere is filtered identically without re-solving it per template — the "filtering layer" the ticket's lede asks for. The block queries the whole collection (tag conditions fit); the authored relationships are pre-selected augmented lists (a modifier fits). The computed flag **is** directly queryable on the current file/Stache driver (verified: 1 eligible of 43 real `people`), so the output filter needs no stored index.

**CP-picker `query_scopes` (AC #4).** Viable but **owned by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405** (blocked on UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919), per the 2026-07-08 UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1361→UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405 split. A `query_scopes` class can filter on the computed flag on the file driver; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405 decides direct in-memory `where` vs. a persisted/indexed mirror for picker efficiency. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 ships **no** stored index — the "queryable/indexed eligible-flag pattern" that note anticipated is, in practice, the in-memory computed-tag condition (`searchable:is`) already in use; no new index was required.

**Schema / JSON-LD `about` (deferred → follow-up).** The only provider-list schema emission is posts' `about` resolving `clinical_providers` through the generic `SchemaThingFromRelationshipResolver` (content `schema_objects` templates). Deferred because a post's `about` is an editorial/historical association ("this article is about Dr. X"), where filtering by *current* eligibility is a distinct product call from live-directory filtering — and applying it means either a cross-repo content-template change or field-specific logic inside a 4,905-line generic resolver.

**Driver-dependency caveat.** The in-memory computed filter (tag condition and modifier) works on the file/Stache driver; migrating `entries` to the eloquent/SQL driver would require a persisted/indexed mirror of the flag for both the output filter and UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405's picker scope.

**Rejected.** *Filter inside the shared `_related_list` partial* — it is entity-agnostic (locations, expertise, conditions, services, resources), so filtering there would wrongly narrow non-provider lists; filter at the call site instead. *Eligibility-only modifier* — would leave actively-separated / hidden providers rendering at the authored sites (AC #2), so the modifier folds in `searchable`. *Flip the block's `$fad_providers_implemented` guard* — UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 supplies the filter, but UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1242 (facet contract) is the remaining guard prerequisite; the guard stays down. *Modify the generic schema resolver for provider eligibility now* — layering violation plus a debatable-benefit editorial-schema filter; deferred.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 issue `## Decision`; `app/Modifiers/ClinicalProviderEligible.php`, `resources/views/page_builder/_uamsweb_block_fad_providers.antlers.html`, `resources/views/clinical_locations/show.antlers.html`, `resources/views/clinical_expertise/{_related,section}.antlers.html`, `resources/views/clinical_resources/_related.antlers.html`. Downstream: UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1405 (CP-picker scope), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1242 (block un-gate), the schema `about` follow-up (resolved 2026-07-15 → UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1473, Preserve). Import-time filtering is `wordpress-importer#19`.

## 2026-07-13 — Site search: one self-contained `/search` page — ranked list + type dropdown, per-type native cards, on the local driver, harvested from Tim Jossund's prototype (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504, #1505, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506)

**Decision.** Ship site search as a **single self-contained `/search` page** on Statamic's **`local`** driver: one **relevance-ranked result list** with a **type `<select>` filter** (All results / People / Clinical Areas of Expertise / Clinical Locations / Clinical Resources / News), each hit rendered through **its content type's existing card partial** — a dynamic `search/cards/{collection}` dispatch to `_fad_provider_card` / `posts/card` / `clinical_locations/card` / `clinical_resources/card`, with a generic fallback card for the rest. The dropdown maps to per-type `local` indexes; "All results" is the unified `default` index. Harvested from Tim Jossund's `feature/search-m` prototype (the results scaffold, the `<select>` type filter, per-result cards) and housekept to app conventions — Bootstrap, the real card partials, a shared `search/_results` listing + `components/pagination`, and a sanitized `q` echo. Both the header search icon and the knowledge-base search bar point here. **Interim shape** — dropdown + per-type native cards; the **ultimate** shape (a typed-tab filter + live type-ahead) is deferred — live type-ahead to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506, the typed-tab filter to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524. This **retires `/search/results`** (the endpoint is now `/search`) and **reverses both the same-day per-type-tabbed plan and the 2026-07-11 "no per-type selector" call for UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815.**

**Why.** A single ranked list plus a type filter is exactly the "unified list with page-type filters, each result a card per type" product direction recorded 2026-07-06 (validated by the 2023 "false floor" finding) — and it is the metaphor Tim's `search-m` prototype already implements, so it reuses his work and credits him. Native card partials render correctly from search rows because Statamic's `Search\Result` proxies augmentation to the underlying entry (computed/asset fields resolve as they do in any collection loop). Tabs are heavier chrome than an MVP needs and forfeit a single cross-type ranking, so they are kept as the *ultimate* target, not the launch shape. `/search` (not `/search/results`) is the canonical URL: the page *is* the search, not a sub-view of it. The dropdown options stay hardcoded in the template because they are coupled in code to the configured indexes in `config/statamic/search.php` (an option whose index is absent throws a 500), carry curated labels the collection titles do not supply ("News" for `posts`), and include the special cross-content "All" scope that is not a collection at all. Zero new engine, package, or infra.

**Rejected.** *Per-type tabbed `/search/results`* (the same-day prior plan) — richer typed navigation, but heavier nav than the MVP needs and it forfeits a single cross-type relevance ranking; kept as the *ultimate* UX (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524, split from UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506), not the launch. *`/search/results` as the URL* — retired; `/search` is the single canonical page. *Flat list without typed cards* — loses the per-type card affordances the 2026-07-06 direction calls for. *Hosted engine (Meilisearch/Typesense)* — parked; `local` suffices at MVP scale, reopened only against the #1505 thresholds. *Live type-ahead dropdown* (Tim's Livewire component) — deferred to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 (drags Livewire onto an Alpine-only front end; a custom Alpine + `fetch` is the future path). *Editor-configurable dropdown via Tim's `search_settings` `collections` global* — his prototype scaffolded it but never wired it; a `collections` field models collections, not search *scopes*, cannot express the "All" scope or the curated labels, and lets an editor pick a collection with no configured index (a runtime 500). Deferred to a dedicated research spike, now filed as #1525 (with Tim's global to be embedded there as prior art). *`get:index` cross-index merge* — never on the table; the filter selects one index per query.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815 (engine/local driver), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#56 (results page → the `/search` page), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#890 (header search icon → `/search` link + remove the empty header tray), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#506 (KB search reuses the shared `search/_results` listing + `components/pagination`), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504 (build PR: `match_weights` tuning + this page + Tim's `users` provisioning), #1505 (launch-scale engine-escalation thresholds), #1525 (the editor-configurable search-scope model — the deferred follow-up, now filed), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 (live type-ahead — now MVP / Project 1), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524 (typed-tab filter — split from UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506). Files: `routes/web.php`, `config/statamic/search.php`, `resources/views/search/` (`index` + `_results` + `cards/`), `resources/views/components/_pagination.antlers.html`, `resources/views/knowledge_bases/_search_bar.antlers.html`. **URL-contract change:** `/search/results?q=` → `/search?q=` — the KB bar is repointed in this PR; future consumers (#1441 C/T redirect, #1230 archive search, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#629 404 box) target `/search`. Tim Jossund's `feature/search-*` branches are preserved as the source; the `match_weights` values and the results/card markup are his (co-authored on the relevant commits).

## 2026-07-14 — Ship the AoE search gate now; carve UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438's subpage indexing to a dedicated follow-up (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1527)

**Decision.** The `searchable` gate / overview / faceted-search core (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1489, and UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438 ACs 1/3/4/5) ships as **PR UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1526**, closing UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060 + UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1489 + UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438's **AC2** — the routed `/{entry}/{section}` virtual subpages appearing as their **own** search results (plus its AC6 subpage-test clause) — is carved out to a **dedicated self-contained issue UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1527**, so UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438 (the shipped gate) closes rather than lingering half-done.

**Why.** AC2 is not a line of code: the subpages are not entries (`ClinicalExpertiseSectionResolver` derives them at request time from one AoE entry), so surfacing them needs a custom `Statamic\Search\Searchables\Provider` synthesizing per-section documents, a reference round-trip, a non-entry render path in `search/_results`, and — the real hazard — `EntrySaved`/`EntryDeleted` listeners to keep those synthetic docs from going stale when an AoE is edited (the `local` driver only reindexes entries). That is an isolable, higher-risk search-infra sub-project (its Jira origin FD20-3307 was a separate task) deserving its own design + adversarial review, not a rushed addition to an otherwise clean, low-risk gate PR. The gate — the reason the bundle waited on site search (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504) — is independently valuable and shippable today.

**Rejected.** *Build the provider in this PR (close all three)* — couples a fragile stale-index mechanism to the gate PR and enlarges the review surface with novel infra under time pressure; deferred to the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438 follow-up. *Expand an overview hit into per-section cards at render time* — does not satisfy AC2 ("as their own results": findable independently, at their own URLs, matchable on section terms) and breaks pagination counts.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438 + UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1527 issue `## Decision` sections; PR UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1526 (the gate). UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1527 carries the custom-`Searchables\Provider` + reindex-sync design.

## 2026-07-14 — AoE/ontology `searchable` gate: a fresh editor toggle, not a revival of the dropped `searchable_query` (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1489)

**Decision.** Deliver UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060's per-entry interactive-search gate as a **new boolean `searchable` toggle** (shared fieldset `uamsweb_entry_search`, `type: toggle`, `default: true`, in a sidebar "Search" section) on the `clinical_expertise`, `clinical_conditions`, and `clinical_services` blueprints. It gates only *interactive* search participation — an AoE overview whose `searchable` is `false` is excluded from the `/search` results (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438), and a condition/service whose `searchable` is `false` no longer surfaces an Area of Expertise through the `/expertise` faceted search (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1489) — while the entry stays published, routable, and in the sitemap. Semantics: an entry participates unless it **explicitly** stores `searchable: false`; missing/null (every existing entry) is treated as searchable, so nothing currently returned silently flips.

**Why.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060 was deferred (2026-07-08) as premature — the gate had "no interactive search surface to attach to" — with the recorded plan to build it *with* that surface. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504 shipped the surface, so building the gate now executes that plan on schedule rather than reopening it. A fresh boolean (not a revival of the removed `searchable_query`) is the honest shape: `searchable_query` was a query-scope *selector* the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#804/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1052 `clinical_condition` normalization deliberately dropped (locked absent by `ClinicalConditionBlueprintTest`'s `it('drops the deprecated fields')`); UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060's need is a simple editor on/off, so a new handle leaves that removal intact — the lock is unchanged. `default: true` + explicit-false-only exclusion keeps the field opt-out and non-breaking. Modeled on the sibling `uamsweb_entry_sitemap` discoverability toggle (same `type: toggle`, `default: true`, sidebar placement).

**Rejected.** *Revive the `searchable_query` field* — reopens a settled normalization removal and drags a query-scope selector where a boolean suffices; the drop-lock stays. *Rename/reuse the existing `people` computed `searchable`* (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 provider-eligibility) — that is a *computed* eligibility flag on a different collection; the clinical gate is an *editor* toggle, so they are distinct fields sharing only the "participates in search" intent. *Default off / exclude on null* — would silently drop every existing published AoE/condition/service from search on deploy; opt-out with an explicit-false gate avoids that. *Gate at render time in `_results`* — breaks pagination counts; the gate belongs at index-build / term-resolution.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1060 issue `## Decision`; `resources/fieldsets/uamsweb_entry_search.yaml`, `resources/blueprints/collections/{clinical_expertise/clinical_expertise,clinical_conditions/clinical_condition,clinical_services/clinical_service}.yaml`, `tests/Feature/Blueprints/ClinicalSearchableFieldTest.php`. Consuming surfaces: UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1438 (`config/statamic/search.php` gate + `app/Scopes`), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1489 (`app/Scopes/ExpertiseByAssociatedOntologySearch.php`). Extends the 2026-07-13 site-search decision (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504).

## 2026-07-14 — Local test-suite slowdown: not UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504; Windows AV ~24% + inherent cost (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1521 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1388)

**Decision.** The ~11× local-CI slowdown observed during UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504's validation is **not** caused by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504's `/search` config — keep `default => 'collection:*'`. The parallel-suite slowdown on Windows decomposes into ~24% Windows Defender real-time scanning (keep the single recursive exclusion of the Herd parked directory) plus a larger **inherent** per-test cost (cold per-worker Stache warm ×12 + per-test churn), tracked in UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1537. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1388's Defender question resolves: keep the exclusion; the sub-path and `php.exe`-process exclusions are no-ops.

**Why.** Microbenchmark on real content: UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504 changed the `default` index from `content` (3,165 docs, 2,088 ms full rebuild) to `collection:*` minus ten collections (151 docs, 117 ms) — an ~18× *faster* rebuild, so it cannot regress the suite; and only `SiteSearchTest` touches search, via a driver-independent `searchables()` path that never builds an on-disk index. A drift-controlled A/B/A on the `Services --parallel --processes=12` probe (reported `Duration`): Defender-excluded ~400–429s vs scanned ~506–533s — non-overlapping bands at two separate times = ~24% AV overhead. The suite still projects to ~55 min *with* the exclusion, so the majority cost is inherent. The original 11× was this inherent Windows cost amplified by a loaded machine (reproduced: a concurrent session's dev server collapsed the per-test rate), exactly the confound UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1521 flagged.

**Rejected.** *Reverting UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504's search config* — exonerated, and `collection:*` is strictly a subset of the prior `content`, so faster. *Adding sub-path (`uams-ci` / `uams-statamic`) or `php.exe`-process Defender exclusions* — proven no-ops (path exclusions are recursive and subsumed by the `Herd` root; the process exclusion had no measurable effect). *Attributing the slowdown to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504* — the search change reduces cost; the slowness predates it (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1381 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1388).

**Companion.** Issues UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1521 (`## Decision`) and UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1388 (`## Decision` + runbook); `docs/runbooks/windows-parallel-test-performance.md`; inherent-cost follow-up UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1537.

## 2026-07-14 — Provider directory (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570) + provider profile (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571): both deferred out of the Find-a-Doc blocks wave

**Decision.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570 and UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571 are deferred out of the Find-a-Doc blocks/templates group and NOT built with UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1468/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1467/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1481/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#62/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#61. Those five ship; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571 return as focused follow-ups. Only the *sequencing* is decided here — the recorded *approaches* (a `people`-slice directory for UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570, reuse of extracted `people/show` partials for UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571) are unchanged.

**Why.** The ground shifted after the 2026-07-11 specs. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070 (shipped) already built the provider-directory *core* — the `/providers` mount + the eligible `people/_listing` card grid — so UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570's remaining work is the *facet layer* on that shipped listing: a large, distinct effort (only 3 of the ~11 WP-parity facets are wired; ratings has no data model and needs an external NRC/DocScores API). UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571 targets `clinical_providers`, an unrouted collection being retired into `people` (#803); the provider profile already renders at `people/{slug}`, so a `clinical_providers/show` template is speculative until #803 settles the consolidation. Deferring keeps the five-ticket wave shippable and sequences the heavy, entangled work behind the facts.

**Rejected.** *Full WP facet parity for UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570 now* — the most valuable option, but a distinct multi-facet build on the shipped core that would balloon an already-large PR (site search UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815 has since shipped, so name search is now buildable, but ratings still needs the external API). *Build only the 3-facet slice (expertise/specialty/region) into this wave* — buildable, but still real added scope on top of five tickets. *Build UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571's `clinical_providers/show` now* — wasted effort if #803 retires the collection.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571 issue `## Decision`; supersedes the *sequencing* (not the approach) of the 2026-07-11 UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#570/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571 entries below; builds on the 2026-07-12 UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070 entry. UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#571 is `blocked_by #803`.

## 2026-07-14 — `/search` polish (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510): keep the `<select>` (tabs stay UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506), card parity with the filter scopes + a strengthened generic card (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506)

**Decision.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510's structure/styling pass over `/search` is two bounded steps, not a redesign. **Controls:** keep the type `<select>` and its single-select semantics — apply on-brand grouping/spacing, a sharper label affordance, and a cleaner empty/pre-query state, preserving UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#815's responsive contract (the field never collapses, the button never wraps). **Cards:** give every *filterable dropdown scope* a real card — add the missing `clinical_expertise` (Areas of Expertise) card so a first-class filter no longer renders through the generic fallback, and refine the existing four (`people` / `posts` / `clinical_locations` / `clinical_resources`) for consistency — while investing the long-tail effort in a **strengthened `search/cards/_default`** (result-type badge / optional thumbnail / cleaner excerpt) that serves the remaining reader-facing types (`pages`, `knowledge_bases`) rather than minting a bespoke card for each. Per-card field lists stay a build-time detail.

**Why.** The control *paradigm* is already pinned (dropdown now; the deferred upgrades are a typed-tab filter (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524) and live type-ahead (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506)), so the only open question was investment level: a chip/segmented rebuild is polish paid twice because UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 replaces the whole affordance, while doing nothing leaves a stock-Bootstrap bar on a flagship page — minimal on-brand polish on the `<select>` is the throwaway-safe fit. For cards, the `default` ("All results") index is `collection:*` minus `ExcludeNonSearchableCollections`, so most collections never reach search and need no card; the real `_default` universe is small (`pages`, `clinical_expertise`, `knowledge_bases`), and the one genuine defect is that `clinical_expertise` is a dropdown filter scope yet has no card. Aligning the *card* set with the *filter* set is a clean, stable line; a strong generic card covers the thin long tail without a maintenance surface that grows with every new collection.

**Rejected.** *Chip / segmented control now* — richer affordance but largely thrown away by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 and borderline-reopens the settled tabs call; deferred into UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506's rebuild. *Defer the controls entirely to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506* — leaves the launch bar visibly basic. *A bespoke card for every surfaced type* — fights the fallback-by-design architecture and grows maintenance with every collection; a good generic card serves the thin types (`pages` / `knowledge_bases`). *Strengthen `_default` only* — under-delivers on the one thing that reads as broken (the AoE scope's generic result). *Tabs at launch / editor-configurable filter options* — already rejected 2026-07-13 (→ UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 / #1505), not reopened here.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510 `## Decision`; extends the 2026-07-13 `/search` entry below. Files: `resources/views/search/index.antlers.html` (controls), `resources/views/search/_results.antlers.html` (dispatch branch), `resources/views/search/cards/` (new `_clinical_expertise`, refined wrappers, strengthened `_default`), `tests/Feature/SiteSearchTest.php` (per-type dispatch + AoE-card coverage). Deferred paradigm shift → UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 (live type-ahead) and UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524 (typed-tab filter). Handle caveat: #1391 (`clinical_expertise` → `clinical_specialties` rename) would move the AoE card's handle.

## 2026-07-14 — Site search: split the deferred "ultimate" shape into live type-ahead (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506) vs. a typed-tab filter (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524); reload-based tabs (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510)

**Decision.** The 2026-07-13 entry's deferred *"ultimate"* shape — "typed tabs + live type-ahead" — resolves into **two independent axes**, tracked as separate tickets that compose but do not block each other:
- **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 — the delivery axis (live type-ahead flyout).** Results stream from the search input as the reader types. A real feature; **MVP scope, on Project 1** (not "post-MVP").
- **UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524 — the filter axis (typed-tab filter).** The interim `<select>` type filter (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1504, polished in UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510) restyled as a content-type tab bar. A **follow-up to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510** (the deferred "tabs-later"), *not* blocked by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506. The active filter is a shared `index` state read by both the static `/search` page and, when present, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506's flyout.

**Tab behavior — reload, not client-side narrow.** A tab click **re-queries the selected per-type `local` index server-side** (a link to `/search?q=…&index=…`), identical in semantics to the shipped `<select>` — matching the reference pattern (search-then-filter reloads with the type in the URL). *Client-side narrowing* is rejected: per-type indexes carry their own `fields` (`people` searches `nickname_list` / `full_name`; `default` only `title` / `page_builder`), so a type-scoped query surfaces matches the "All" page never had, and results are paginated — hiding DOM rows would show a wrong, truncated set. The **no-reload AJAX swap** (`fetch` + `@alpinejs/morph`) is deferred to ride UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506's engine (the delivery axis), not built on the tabs. Browser-history `replaceState` (Back returns to pre-search) is a nice-to-have on UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524.

**Why.** Type-ahead and the tab filter are orthogonal — filter *presentation* (`<select>` → tabs) and result *delivery* (submit-to-page → live flyout) are different concerns that compose. Splitting keeps UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 a crisp, shippable feature and gives the tabs their own tidy ticket without a false `blocked_by`. Tim Jossund's prototype (embedded in UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506) implements both together in one Livewire component, but as reference-only prior art (Livewire is a deliberately-avoided dependency), so there is no mergeable increment to carve off by provenance.

**Rejected.** *Fold both into UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506* — makes UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 a two-phase epic, muddies "done". *`blocked_by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506` on the tabs* — the tabs filter the static page independently of live search, so the edge would be false. *Client-side tab narrowing* — semantically wrong (per-index fields) and breaks pagination. *AJAX tab swap now* — duplicates UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506's engine on the filter axis; deferred to ride it.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 `## Decision` (live type-ahead, MVP), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1524 `## Decision` (typed-tab filter, reload-based), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1510 (the interim `<select>` these evolve). Prior art embedded in UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1506 (Tim's `dropdown.blade.php`). Files (future): `resources/views/search/index.antlers.html`, `config/statamic/search.php`, `@alpinejs/morph`.

## 2026-07-15 — Standardize the cover-image field set: Replace + consolidate on `pages` + `knowledge_bases` (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1553)

**Decision.** Consolidate `pages/{basic_page,landing_page}` and `knowledge_bases/{knowledge_base,article,topic}` off the legacy single `featured_image` (a `uamsweb_entry_meta` handle) onto the standard 3-handle cover-image set (`featured_image_16_9` / `_4_3` / `_1_1`) — the 16:9 left **optional** (dimensions-only, no `required`). Keep `excerpt`; point the KB `_article_card` / `_topic_list` thumbnails at `featured_image_16_9`; rewire the shared `_seo_meta` og:image to `featured_image_16_9 ?: featured_image ?: spotlight_featured_image`. `clinical_locations` stays on its single `featured_image` (covered by the fallback). The WordPress importer remap is `UAMS-Web/wordpress-importer#433`.

**Why.** `pages` and `knowledge_bases` were the same single-handle case (an earlier draft of UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1553 mis-stated pages had no featured image — they carry one via `uamsweb_entry_meta`, the live `og:image` source via `_seo_meta`), so consolidating them together onto the editorial-standard shape is coherent, and it is what the schema.org base-webpage template — already reading `featured_image_16_9|featured_image|thumbnail` — expects. The 16:9 is optional because these collections routinely have entries with no hero; a required one would block saving them.

**Rejected.** *Additive (keep the single handle, add the set alongside)* — two featured-image concepts per entry, defeats the one-convention goal. *Leave pages/KB as-is (they already carry a featured image)* — underserves UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1532's entry-sourced cards, which render `featured_image_16_9` / `_4_3`. *Also convert `clinical_locations`* — over-engineering: single-image card, bespoke parent-override logic, no aspect-ratio need. *Require the 16:9 (matching `posts` / `clinical_expertise`)* — blocks saving the many existing hero-less entries.

**Companion.** Issue UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1553 `## Decision`; importer remap `UAMS-Web/wordpress-importer#433`; precedent UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1378. Corrects the shared "pages has no featured image field" premise, also carried by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1532.

## 2026-07-15 — Recognition-list membership: re-model to a structured collection with two-way stillat sync (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1546 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1176)

**Decision.** Re-model provider ↔ recognition-list membership: convert `recognition_lists` from a flat taxonomy into a **structured collection** (a list is a top-level entry; its categories are child entries), and hold membership as a **two-way `manyToMany` relationship** synced by `stillat/relationships` (`people.recognition_lists ⇄ recognition_lists.members`). UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1176's provider-block recognition filter rides on this as a `whereJsonOverlaps` once the top-level relationship exists. Tracked in UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1546 (blocks UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1176).

**Why.** The filter (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1176) needs queryable membership, and new rosters are authored in bulk from the list side (dozens of people at once). Today `people.recognition_lists` is a **replicator** (nested `recognition_list` term + free-text `category` + `enabled`) — not queryable (`whereJsonOverlaps` can't reach nested slugs), `people` doesn't attach the `recognition_lists` taxonomy (no reverse index), and `stillat/relationships` can only sync **top-level** relationship fields (it can't hook a term inside a replicator set, nor write the replicator's set scaffolding). A structured collection is the only shape that satisfies hierarchical categories (a category *within* a list), authoring from either end, and queryable membership all at once. The replicator's `category` / `enabled` metadata is currently rendered nowhere, so the migration relocates it without breaking output.

**Rejected.** *Keep the replicator + per-render scan scope* — O(all people) each uncached render, novel pattern, no category or list-side authoring. *Keep the replicator + one-way listener-maintained derived flat field* — drift-prone, one-directional (no bulk list-side authoring), categories not first-class. *Keep `recognition_lists` a taxonomy + a `members` field on the term* (stillat `entry↔term`) — queryable two-way without a collection migration, but **taxonomy terms are flat in Statamic**, so a category can't live *within* a list. *Nest `members` in a Grid / Replicator on the list entry* — stillat can't sync a relationship nested in a replicator row, killing the person→lists direction.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1546 (the re-model, `afk`) blocks UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1176 (the filter). Precedent: UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1433 (`post_categories` taxonomy → structured collection, Wave 122); Wave 73 (stillat `manyToMany` ontology sync).

**Build residuals (confirmed, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1546).** The list's `series` / `publication` / `data_source` classification stays as taxonomy relationships on the list-entry blueprint (not folded into the structure tree); the surviving sub-taxonomies still carry it. The per-membership `enabled` flag is **dropped** (it rendered nowhere; entry-level `active` governs a list's visibility). Category child entries are deduped per list by (list slug + slugified category name). The collection is **`default`-site only** — recognition lists are national/canonical and the sole data-bearing provider is default-only; a `health`-site expansion is a clean follow-up if editorial need arises. The list-taxonomy `award_name` augmentation is retired (rendered nowhere); the series `full_name` computed is kept. **Importer parity descoped** to `UAMS-Web/wordpress-importer#434` (`hitl`): the importer imports no recognition data today, so populating the new model is net-new work that reopens the 2026-07-07 match-only decision — a fork tracked there, not in UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1546.

## 2026-07-15 — Departmental News Hub: a department is a multisite site; opt-in site-scoped template (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1160)

**Decision.** A "department" is already a first-class **multisite site** (255-site multisite; ~218 entity sites, e.g. `medicine_anesthesiology` at `/medicine/anesthesiology`), so the Departmental News Hub is an **opt-in, site-scoped landing template** (real-multisite, `Site::current()`) — not `organizations`-routing on the main site and not a new `departments` collection. Spoke Feed = the existing UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#76 news block (`source="current"`; `posts` is per-site, `propagate:false`); schema anchor = reuse the per-site `schema_subject`; people/faculty zone = **derived from the existing `job_title` role/title replicator** (each role set already links to an `organizations` entry, leadership-flagged), parameterized by the site's org type via a small org-role → role-set mapping. The one genuinely missing link is a site→`organizations` bridge. Adopted slices: UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1555 (template design spike), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1556 (site→org bridge + `schema_subject` enforcement), UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1557 (people zone).

**Why.** The multisite already models departments as sites with per-site chrome (`uams_multisite_settings`) and per-site news; rebuilding that as routed `organizations` data on one site duplicates it ~218-fold. `job_title` already carries the person→department academic/clinical/institutional appointment graph, so no new faculty model is needed.

**Rejected.** *`organizations`-entry anchor + main-site routing, or a new `departments` collection* — duplicates the institutional-department structure the multisite provides; the department **is** a site. *Curated landing page per site* — drifts and defeats the consistent-node thesis at ~218 sites; template + thin curated fieldset instead. *Systematic all-sites rollout* — opt-in instead; not every site is a department. *New post→org association / taxonomy* — unneeded; posts are per-site. *New person→department appointment model* — unneeded; `job_title` already models it. Child-unit roll-up deferred.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1160 issue `## Decision`; follow-ups UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1555 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1556 / UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1557. Verified against `resources/fieldsets/uamsweb_common_people.yaml` (`job_title`), `resources/sites.yaml`, `uams_multisite_settings`, the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#76 news block.

## 2026-07-15 — Post schema `about` provider list: Preserve, not filtered by eligibility (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1473)

**Decision.** A post's schema/JSON-LD `about` `clinical_providers` list is **not** filtered by clinical-provider eligibility. Resolves the schema-`about` leg deferred in the 2026-07-12 UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 entry below.

**Why.** `about` is an editorial/historical factual statement, not a live "who can I see now" directory; eligibility is time-variant (lost on separation/role change without the post re-saving), so filtering would silently drop a validly-referenced subject from an article's structured data on an unrelated HR event. An `about` triple carries no "book an ineligible provider" harm the way a directory listing does, so filtering degrades the data without protecting anyone.

**Rejected.** *Filter via an `eligible_clinical_providers_only` tag param* — clean and reuses UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919's predicates, but over-filters editorial truth; kept only as a fallback if a hard "never name an ineligible provider in any machine-readable surface" rule is adopted. *Filter via a resolver seam keyed on provider field handles* — field-handle special-casing in the generic ~4,900-line `SchemaThingFromRelationshipResolver` (a wart); rejected outright.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1473 issue `## Decision`; resolves the schema-`about` follow-up of the 2026-07-12 UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#919 decision.

## 2026-07-15 — Entry-level featured-image media-credit control: scope + defaults (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1532)

**Decision.** The entry-level featured-image credit control (a `true_false` field wired into entry-sourced blocks) covers `posts`, `clinical_resources`, `knowledge_bases`, and `pages`; **default hidden** (opt in only when the asset's license requires it); presentation is a small inline `.photo-credit` line under the thumbnail (reusing UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1530/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1531's `_media_credit` partial), not an overlay. Precedence is a non-conflict — a linked-entry image honors this entry-level control, an author-chosen image honors the block-level toggle. `pages` has no featured image today, so this is **blocked by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1553** (roll the standard cover-image set onto `pages`, mirroring UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1378).

**Why.** These four collections surface editorial cover media that can carry third-party attribution; `people` is excluded (portraits/headshots aren't editorial credit surfaces).

**Rejected.** *Include `people`* — crediting a provider's own headshot on a card is noise. *Default shown* — over-surfaces credits the license doesn't require. *Overlay on cards* — competes with dense card text.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1532 issue `## Decision`; blocked by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1553; depends on UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1530/UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1531's `_media_credit` partial.

## 2026-07-15 — Routed provider `/{section}` subpages: extract a shell + two section strategies (#1475)

**Decision.** Generalize the AoE routed-section subsystem by **extracting a collection-agnostic dispatch shell** (route-`{section}`-constraint union, controller dispatch, template-by-collection) and keeping AoE's section-map/chrome as one strategy while adding a thin **flat** provider strategy over `people`'s relationship fields. Not an in-place rewrite, not a duplicated parallel subsystem.

**Why.** The two collections share the route+controller shell but diverge internally — AoE is structured (ancestor walks, `@children` specialties, inherited CTA); providers are flat. The shell/strategy split keeps DRY where shared and isolation where different, leaving AoE's 246-line resolver internals and the ~46 tests guarding them untouched.

**Rejected.** *Generalize in place* — most DRY but rewrites structure assumptions under a just-shipped feature (highest regression risk). *Parallel provider subsystem* — zero AoE risk but duplicates the route/controller and forks the single-sourced route constraint.

**Companion.** #1475 issue `## Decision`. Moved to Post-MVP (project 3). Follow-up to UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1070; AoE subsystem `ClinicalExpertiseSection{Resolver,Controller}` + `ClinicalExpertiseComputedFieldsService`.

## 2026-07-15 — Marketing-redirect approval: a `status` field on the redirect row (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1357)

**Decision.** Approval state is a `status` field (`pending` / `approved` / `rejected` + reason) on the redirect row; the serve gate requires `status === approved` beside the existing per-row `enabled` flag (both in `FindRedirectRule`). A new `approve marketing redirects` permission (distinct from create; no self-approve for MVP); pending count surfaced on the CP utility (email push a fast-follow); scheduling reuses UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1356's validity window.

**Why.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1355 stores redirects as replicator rows in a Statamic global set gated by `enabled`; a `status` field is native to that shape and gates serving with one added check — no second collection, no dual-write.

**Rejected.** *A separate "redirect request" collection that writes into the store on approval* — heavier, mismatches the single-global-set storage, and adds a promotion/drift seam.

**Build notes (2026-07-16, PR UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1608).** Three load-bearing implementation choices the build settled: (1) the serve gate **grandfathers a missing/unknown `status` as `approved`**, so legacy globals-editor rows and WordPress-importer rows (no `status` key) keep serving — the field is additive, never a silent regression. (2) Collision detection (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1356) is **status-aware at the call site**: rejected rows are excluded (they never serve, so must not reserve a source — otherwise the reject → correct → resubmit loop deadlocks), while approved, legacy, and still-pending rows continue to collide (adversarial-review finding). (3) The importer stamps its own explicit `status: approved` via `UAMS-Web/wordpress-importer#440` so the migration mapping is explicit rather than only grandfathered.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1357 issue `## Decision`; blocked by UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1355. Part of the #1105 redirect-utility decomposition. Importer mapping follow-up: `UAMS-Web/wordpress-importer#440`.

## 2026-07-15 — Public redirect-intake form: deferred (#1358)

**Decision.** Do **not** build the optional public intake form now (deferred, not rejected). Revisit if KB-request volume exceeds manual capacity or product asks once UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1357 ships; if built, honeypot + `throttle` + strict pending-only (a public submission may only ever create a *pending* request, never a live redirect).

**Why.** Explicitly optional fourth slice, two dependencies deep (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1357 unbuilt, UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1355 unmerged), no demonstrated demand, and the app has been tightening public write surfaces (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#863), not opening them. The safe design is known; the real fork was "is a new public write surface worth it," and the answer for now is no.

**Rejected.** *Build now* — premature, unproven demand, added attack surface.

**Companion.** #1358 issue `## Decision`. Moved to Post-MVP (project 3). Composes with the #1105 open-redirect trust boundary (2026-07-06).

## 2026-07-15 — Location tailored contact options (Mitel/ABC): model now, global-widget + location param (#1211)

**Decision.** Model per-location tailored contact options on `clinical_locations` now — a channel `select` + an opaque routing-parameter/queue-code field + optional label, rendered by `_profile_details` with the generic phone treatment as the unset default, in the **global-widget + location-parameter** shape (the `mychart_scheduling` precedent). Ship phone / form / external-link now; **gate live-chat rendering** on the external Mitel/ABC routing contract (a named external dependency).

**Why.** The location model + partial are local and extensible; the ABC queue-routing values and widget wiring are owned by Mitel/ABC and not yet defined, so the model ships now and live-chat is populated once that contract lands.

**Rejected.** *Per-location raw embed* (`type: code` per location) — N scripts to sync + a per-entry raw-HTML XSS surface. *Defer entirely* — needlessly blocks the buildable local model on external setup.

**Companion.** #1211 issue `## Decision`. Moved to Post-MVP (project 3). Source: Jira `FD20-370`.

## 2026-07-15 — CI jobs run as a cost-ordered sequential chain, not in parallel (budget over wall-clock; supersedes UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1543)

**Decision.** The CI jobs — in [`.github/workflows/ci.yml`](../.github/workflows/ci.yml) and its [`scripts/local-ci.mjs`](../scripts/local-ci.mjs) mirror — run as a strict sequential chain ordered shortest → longest: `isolation → encoding → spell → js → build → pint → larastan → phpunit → browser` (browser opt-in, last), each gated on the previous via `needs:`. A failure at any step skips — and so never bills Actions minutes for — every more-expensive step after it. Supersedes the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1543 spell-only gate.

**Why.** GitHub bills runner-minutes summed across jobs, and a gated-out job costs zero. The two expensive jobs — Larastan and Pest (>2 min each, Pest the longest) — dominate the budget, so the goal is to guarantee that *any* earlier failure (a 16s isolation guard, a 35s spelling typo, a Pint formatting slip) skips them. Actions budget is the binding constraint here; wall-clock on the common success path is not — remote CI is a gated kill-switch (`RUN_REMOTE_CI`) and validation runs locally — so paying serialized wall-clock on green runs to save the expensive-job minutes on red runs is the right trade for this repo.

**Rejected.** *Fully parallel (pre-#1543)* — every job always runs and bills even when a 16s guard already failed; zero failure-path savings. *Spell-only gate (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1543)* — only a cspell failure skipped the heavy jobs; a Pint / JS / build failure still paid for Larastan + Pest. *Two-tier gate (cheap jobs parallel → Larastan/Pest/browser `needs:` all of them)* — captures most of the savings and keeps the happy path parallel (wall-clock = max, not sum), but runs a more complex mirror and still pays for every *cheap* job when one of them fails; the strict chain was chosen for maximal, simplest failure-path savings and one-failure-at-a-time clarity, accepting the serial happy-path wall-clock as the deliberate cost.

**Companion.** Branch `ci-sequential-job-chain` — `ci.yml`'s `needs:` graph + `scripts/local-ci.mjs`'s `CHAIN` loop (runs in order, stops at the first failure). Supersedes the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1543 spell-only gate; the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1018 advisory → blocking spell history and the `RUN_REMOTE_CI` kill-switch are unchanged.

## 2026-07-16 — Per-test multisite site hydration: cache the hydrated collection once per process (the UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1537 boot lever) (UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1582)

**Decision.** Cache the hydrated 255-site `resources/sites.yaml` collection once per PHP process in the test harness and inject it into every later per-test app boot, via a test-only `Tests\Support\CachedSites` subclass of `Statamic\Sites\Sites` rebound onto `Sites::class` in a `CreatesApplication` `booting()` callback. Only the hydrated *collection* is cached, not the `Sites` instance, so the per-request mutable state a `Sites` carries (`$current`, `$currentUrlCallback`) stays fresh per app. Removes ~280ms/boot (~33% of boot; ~236ms drift-controlled interleaved) — the app-boot lever from UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1537 (boot = 45% of parallel CPU; the site hydration ≈ 79% of that boot). No CI wiring or artisan command (unlike the sibling UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1581): the cache is purely in-process and lazily populated, so there is no pre-run artifact to build.

**Why.** A hydrated `Site` holds only resolved scalars/arrays (`handle`, `config`, `rawConfig`, `isDefault`) — no app or container reference — and every test app resolves identical URLs from a fixed `config('app.url')`, so the collection is read-only global config, safe to share; verified by a reflection property audit plus byte-identical resolved config across independent hydrations. `booting()` is the injection point because it fires after Statamic's `AppServiceProvider::register()` binds `Sites::class` and before the Stache boot resolves `Site::all()`.

**Rejected.** (a) Reusing the booted app across methods — stays rejected per UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1121 (forfeits Laravel's per-method isolation; blast radius the whole suite). (b) Sharing the whole `Sites` singleton instance — leaks `$current` / `$currentUrlCallback` between tests, so cache only the collection. (c) Injecting the cached collection into a genuine `Sites` via reflection (`newInstanceWithoutConstructor` + set the protected `$sites`) to keep the `Site` facade root a real `Sites` — rejected as more magic than an idiomatic subclass; the subclass IS-A `Sites` and is transparent to `instanceof` and method calls. (d) A `final` subclass — breaks Mockery for the seven tests that mock the `Site` facade (Mockery cannot subclass a `final` facade root), so the subclass is non-`final` with a documented reason and a regression guard.

**Companion.** UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1582 issue `## Decision`; UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1581 (sibling warm lever, PR UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1590); UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1537 (parent attribution); UAMS-Web/uams-statamic-archive#1121 (app-reuse rejected). `tests/Support/CachedSites.php`, `tests/CreatesApplication.php`.
